研 究  報 告                
中砂(1560.TT) 
產業：電機機械 
上修資本支出用於擴產隱含展望正向，看好毛利率延續成⻑趨勢 
 投資評價與建議 
3Q26毛利率表現優於預期，且公司上修資本支出用於擴產，隱含主要業務展
望正向，加上公司積極優化產品結構隱含明年營收、獲利均具上修空間；我們
給予40倍PE，維持對公司業務之正向看法，並看好毛利率維持成⻑動能。 
 重點評論分析 
4Q26三大業務營收 、 獲利持續改善： 觀察ABU業務中 ， 預期高階砂輪與ABF鑽
⽯機密模具需求持續成⻑，DBU業務出貨動能則維持強勁，加上公司規劃針對
SBU部分品項漲價約10%，將帶動營收、毛利率改善。我們預估本季營收有望
季增5.5%至28.4億元，毛利率44.5%，稅後EPS 4.37元。 
上修今年資本支出預算用於擴產與新品開發，隱含營運前景正向：基於DBU、
SBU客戶需求強勁，及公司持續優化產品組合，公司上修今年資本支出預算至
22.6億元(原16~17億元)，除了先前已規畫SBU 3Q27月產能至43萬片(已宣
布)，本次則上修今明年底鑽⽯碟月產能分別至8、11萬顆(原7萬顆以上/9.5萬
顆) ， 此外將購買土地廠房用於開發先進封裝用之減薄鑽⽯砂輪新品 ， 以及後續
鑽⽯碟擴產需求。而在2027年展望方面，我們仍維持2027年三大業務均正向
成⻑之看法，其中DBU、SBU成⻑動能最為強勁，另ABU業務中，公司布局之
先進封裝用之減薄砂輪有望於2H27貢獻營收。預估2027年營收年增25.7%至
129.7億元，毛利率46%，稅後EPS 21.7元。 
 投資風險 
DBU擴產進度不如預期。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況  
3Q26毛利率表現優於預期：3Q26營收季增8.1%至26.9億元，毛利率42.2%，稅後EPS 3.88元，其中毛利率受惠
於：(1)台灣工具機客戶需求回溫、ABF鑽⽯機密模具用量增加，帶動ABU高階產品比重提升，挹注該業務毛利率
由2Q26的超過25%提升至3Q26的35% ；(2)針對DBU大客戶之2/1.6奈米 、先進封裝鑽⽯碟用量持續增加， 使營收
規模擴大，我們推估毛利率由2Q26的50~55%提升至55%以上。 
4Q26三大業務營收、獲利持續改善：觀察：(1)ABU業務中，預期高階砂輪與ABF鑽⽯機密模具需求持續成⻑，至
於泰國、日本子公司出貨動能則持平看待，預估營收微幅季增，毛利率則有望持續向上；(2)DBU業務中，出貨動
能則維持強勁，因台系大客戶先進製程、封裝需求維持強勁，另美系記憶體、IDM客戶需求呈現逐季成⻑，加上
成熟製程廠商產能利用率持續提升，預估營收仍有雙位數季增；(3)SBU業務中，12吋月產能維持40萬片滿載，但
公司計畫針對部分品項價格調漲約10%，加上持續優化產品組合，將帶動營收、毛利率改善。預估本季營收有望
季增5.5%至28.4億元，毛利率44.5%，稅後EPS 4.37元。 
上修今年資本支出預算用於擴產與新品開發，隱含營運前景正向：基於DBU、SBU客戶需求強勁，及公司持續優
化產品組合，公司上修今年資本支出預算至22.6億元(原16~17億元)，除了先前已規畫SBU 3Q27月產能至43萬片
(已宣布)，本次則上修今明年底鑽⽯碟月產能分別至8、11萬顆(原7萬顆以上/9.5萬顆)，此外將購買土地廠房用於
開發先進封裝用之減薄鑽⽯砂輪新品，以及後續鑽⽯碟擴產需求。而在2027年展望方面，我們仍維持2027年三大
業務均正向成⻑之看法 ， 動能包含 ： (1)預估DBU主要客戶A16/2 、 3奈米明年底月產能將由今年底的9~10 、 15~17
分別提升至14~16、19~20萬片，尤其先進製程CMP道數、規格相較於成熟製程提升，將帶動鑽⽯碟用量、平均
單價增加，另美系IDM合作順利(目前公司於18A市占約50%，公司持續推進14A之合作)，加上成熟製程、記憶體
客戶擴產挹注 ； (2)SBU除了有明年額外3萬片之產能挹注 ， 公司針對現有產能也將持續透過產品組合優化等方式改
善獲利，且看好2H27美系記憶體特殊晶圓用量將比1H27成⻑；(3)ABU方面，則基於台灣ABF廠商持續擴產，公
司布局之新品先進封裝減薄砂輪有望在2H27於新廠貢獻營收，加上公司將中低毛利率產品搬至泰國、日本子公司
生產以此優化成本結構。我們預估2027年營收年增25.7%至129.7億元，毛利率46%，稅後EPS 21.7元。

### Page 2

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引

1

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國就業及企業活動持續改善，期中選舉牽動財政與科技監管政策 
經濟 
⚫ 10 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數由 54.5 升至 55.5，連續第 10 個
月維持於擴張區間；新訂單、生產及庫存表現改善，顯示需求與生產動
能仍具韌性。惟投入成本仍處高檔，且關稅政策、地緣政治及供應鏈風
險猶存，後續發展仍有待進一步觀察。 
⚫ 10 月美國 ISM 服務業採購經理人指數為 54.5，較上月 54.9 略降 0.4 個
百分點，但已連續 28 個月維持擴張，服務業景氣持續增長。新訂單與
商業活動雖趨緩 ， 就業續呈擴張 ， 未完成訂單創2022 年 7 月以來高點 ；
新出口訂單重返擴張區間，顯示內外需求仍具韌性，惟關稅、能源價格
及成本壓力仍為後續觀察重點。 
⚫ 10 月美國非農就業增加 16.5 萬人，高於上月修正後之 6.0 萬人，失業
率由 4.2%降至 4.1%，加以前兩個月就業人數合計上修 8.4 萬人，顯示
勞動市場較前期有所回溫。惟新增就業仍主要集中於專業及商業服務、
醫療保健及運輸倉儲等部分服務業，製造業增幅相對有限，反映勞動市
場雖維持韌性，但部分就業品質及企業用工需求仍存在壓力。 
⚫ 9 月美國職位空缺數由 719.0 萬個增至 752.3 萬個，職缺率由 4.3%升至
4.5%，企業徵才需求有所回升；聘僱人數亦同步增加，聘僱率由 3.3%
升至 3.4%。整體而言，企業用人需求改善、裁員規模下降，勞動市場仍
維持穩健，供需情勢持續朝平衡方向發展。 
⚫ 9 月美國商品及服務貿易逆差 1,050 億美元，較上月減少 0.7%。出口成
長 1.9%，高於進口增幅 1.2%，主因資本財及黃豆等農產品出口增加；
進口則受資本財及消費財需求帶動 。 累計1 至 9 月貿易逆差較去年同期
縮減 12.2%，顯示對外貿易失衡情況有所改善。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 2026 年美國期中選舉呈現反執政黨效應 ， 民主黨重掌國會主導權 。 截至
11 月 8 日開票結果，民主黨已確定取得參、眾兩院多數席次，其中參議
院獲 54 席、共和黨 44 席；眾議院則由民主黨取得 254 席、共和黨 162
席。州長席次方面，民主黨掌握28 席，共和黨 20 席，惟部分選區仍在
計票，最終席次尚待確認。民主黨在多個關鍵州選戰中獲勝，反映選民
對高物價、能源價格及中東局勢等議題的不滿情緒升高。選後國會由民

用相關內容。

2

主黨主導，將對川普政府形成較大制衡，未來財政、稅制及貿易政策推
動難度提高；雖有助抑制財政赤字擴張，但預算協商及債務上限議題之
不確定性可能升高。 
⚫ 美國民主黨於期中選舉後取得國會主導權，人工智慧監管政策受到關
注。參議員桑德斯等人提出禁止人工超級智慧法案，主張限制先進 AI
研發、設立聯邦人工智慧部，並加強安全監管。該法案尚未通過，後續
須關注立法進展及對科技產業的影響。 
短評 
⚫ 美國就業市場回穩，製造業與服務業持續擴張，顯示經濟仍具韌性，惟
產業表現不均、企業成本壓力偏高，通膨風險尚未消除。另一方面，貿
易逆差雖略有改善，但關稅及供應鏈不確定性仍存，加上期中選舉改變
國會權力結構 ， 未來財政 、 貿易及AI 監管政策恐面臨更多協商與制衡 ，
後續須關注政策變化對企業投資、經濟成長及金融市場之影響。 
歐 
元 
區 
通膨壓力持續增強 
經濟 
⚫ 歐元區 10 月 HICP 通膨上升 0.2 個百分點至 4%。 
⚫ 歐元區 9 月失業率上升 0.1 個百分點為 6.5%。 
⚫ 歐元區 9 月零售額月減 0.7%、第三季季減 0.5%。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 為打擊從中國湧入的低價電商品，歐盟自 11 月 1 日起正式落實對境外
包裹加收 2 歐元處理費的措施；此外，歐盟執委會於 11 月 4 日正式提
出研究制定 「地緣經濟威懾協定」 ，以限制並反制中國對歐盟的貿易威
脅。

一、 美國—就業及企業活動持續改善，期中選舉牽動財政與科技監管政策 
2026 年 10 月美國非農就業人數增加 16.5 萬人，明顯高於上月修正後之 6.0
萬人，亦高於過去 12 個月平均每月增加 4.8 萬人之水準，企業聘僱動能有所回
升；失業率則由4.2%降至 4.1%，持續維持於 3 月以來 4.1%至 4.3%之區間，勞動
市場整體仍具穩定性。產業別方面，新增就業主要由專業及商業服務業增加 7.0
萬人、醫療保健業增加 4.2 萬人及運輸倉儲業增加 2.5 萬人所帶動，製造業亦增
加 1.9 萬人，其餘產業就業變動幅度相對有限。薪資方面，私人部門平均時薪升
至 38.0 美元，月增 0.4%、年增 3.1%，增幅較前月略有加快；平均每週工時則維
持 34.4 小時不變。此外，8 月及 9 月新增就業人數合計上修 8.4 萬人，反映上兩
月勞動市場表現較初估時有所改善。整體而言，10 月美國新增就業明顯回升、失
業率小幅下降，加上前兩個月就業數據獲得上修，勞動市場有所改善，整體仍具
韌性；惟新增就業主要集中於部分服務業，且因經濟因素從事兼職工作的人數增
加，反映勞動市場仍存在就業不足及產業表現不均的情況。 
2026 年 9 月美國職位空缺數(Job Openings) 由上月修正後之 719.0 萬個增加
33.3 萬個至 752.3 萬個，職缺率由4.3%升至 4.5%，企業徵才需求略有回溫；聘僱
人數由 527.1 萬人增加至 540.2 萬人，聘僱率由 3.3%升至 3.4%。總離職人數由
517.5 萬人增加至 531.9 萬人，離職率維持於 3.3%。就離職結構觀察，主動離職
(Quits)人數由 304.4 萬人增加至 336.5 萬人，主動離職率由 1.9%升至 2.1%，勞工
轉職意願有所回升；裁員與解僱(Layoffs and Discharges)人數則由 176.8 萬人減少
至 164.1 萬人，裁員率由1.1%降至 1.0%，企業人力調整壓力仍屬溫和。產業別方
面，職位空缺增加主要來自州及地方政府 (不含教育)、專業與商業服務業、製造
業及營建業；聘僱人數則以住宿與餐飲服務業增幅較為明顯；醫療保健與社會救
助業裁員人數則有所下降。整體而言，9 月美國職缺數與聘僱人數同步增加，主
動離職意願回升且裁員規模下降，顯示企業用人需求有所改善，勞動市場仍維持
穩健，惟供需關係持續朝較為平衡方向發展。 
2026 年 10 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI) 由上月的
54.5 升至 55.5，增加 1.0 點，連續第 10 個月維持於 50 以上擴張區間，製造業景

氣持續擴張。細項觀察，新訂單指數由55.3 升至 55.5，接單情況持續改善；生產
指數由 56.7 攀升至 59.0， 製造活動進一步增溫 ； 就業指數雖由52.7 微降至 52.4，
但仍維持擴張。供應商交貨指數由 59.0 微升至 59.1，反映交貨時間持續延長；庫
存指數由 48.6 升至 51.4，顯示企業備貨意願回升。此外，價格指數由 77.9 降至
75.2， 投入成本壓力雖略有緩和但仍處高檔 ； 未完成訂單指數雖由56.4 降至 54.6，
但仍在擴張區間。另一方面，新出口訂單指數由50.9 升至 51.2，進口指數由 51.0
升至 52.9，均維持擴張區間，顯示外需及企業採購需求仍具支撐。整體而言，10
月美國製造業活動持續穩健擴張，新訂單、生產及庫存表現改善，顯示需求與生
產動能仍具韌性；惟投入成本仍處相對高檔，加上關稅政策、地緣政治情勢及供
應鏈變數猶存，後續對企業成本控制及製造業景氣發展之影響仍值得持續關注。 
2026 年 10 月美國 ISM 服務業採購經理人指數(Services PMI)為 54.5，較上月
54.9 下降 0.4 個百分點，惟已連續第 28 個月維持於 50 以上擴張區間，且高於近
12 個月平均值 54.3，美國服務業景氣持續擴張。細項觀察，商業活動指數由56.5
降至 53.8，新訂單指數由 59.8 降至 55.9，顯示需求與營運動能較前月放緩，但仍
維持擴張；就業指數由 50.1 升至 51.4，連續第 2 個月處於擴張區間，反映企業用
人需求仍具韌性。供應商交貨指數由 53.2 升至 56.7，交貨時間進一步延長；價格
指數由 74.0 降至 73.0，雖較前月回落，但已連續 23 個月高於 60，企業成本壓力
仍高。庫存指數由 57.8 降至 55.4，未完成訂單指數則由 56.6 升至 58.0，創 2022
年 7 月以來最高，顯示需求累積情形持續增加。此外，新出口訂單指數由46.9 大
幅升至 55.2，重返擴張區間，進口指數亦由52.9 升至 54.2，顯示外部需求及採購
活動回溫。ISM 指出，關稅及燃料成本仍是企業最關注的供應鏈議題，許多企業
尚未完全將增加成本轉嫁給客戶，未來仍須持續關注關稅、能源價格及成本壓力
對服務業景氣與通膨之影響；惟值得注意的是，10 月所有主要指標均位於擴張區
間，為 2026 年 2 月以來首見，顯示整體服務業景氣基礎仍具支撐。 
2026 年 9 月美國商品及服務貿易逆差為 1,050 億美元，較上月減少 7 億美元
(-0.7%)。當月出口 3,215 億美元，較上月增加58 億美元(+1.9%)；進口 4,266 億美
元，增加 52 億美元(+1.2%)，出口增幅高於進口，使貿易逆差略為改善。其中，
商品貿易逆差縮減至 1,359 億美元(-0.5%)，服務貿易順差略降至 309 億美元(-
0.1%)。出口成長主要受資本財出口增加 38 億美元帶動，其中電腦、電腦周邊設
備及民用航空器表現較佳 ； 食品、 飼料及飲料出口亦增加19 億美元 ， 以黃豆增幅

最為顯著。進口方面，資本財進口增加116 億美元，消費財進口增加24 億美元，
惟工業用品及原材料進口減少 37 億美元。累計 2026 年 1 至 9 月，美國商品及服
務貿易逆差為 6,623 億美元，較 2025 年同期減少-12.2%；同期出口成長 11.6%，
進口增加 6.2%，顯示對外貿易失衡情況較去年同期有所改善。 
2026 年美國期中選舉呈現反執政黨效應 ， 民主黨重掌國會主導權 ， 截至2026
年 11 月 8 日，美國期中選舉開票結果顯示，民主黨已確定取得國會參、眾兩院
控制權。其中，參議院民主黨陣營取得 54 席，共和黨 44 席；眾議院民主黨取得
254 席，共和黨 162 席。此外，全美州長席次分布方面，民主黨掌握28 席，共和
黨 20 席 。由於部分選區仍在計票或尚未確認選舉結果 ， 最終席次仍待確定 。 民主
黨並於全國眾議院普選票(Popular Vote) 獲得較高得票率，且在德州、堪薩斯州、
北卡羅來納州、俄亥俄州及南卡羅來納州等關鍵州選戰取得突破，反映選民對高
物價、能源價格上漲、中東衝突及部分政策方向的不滿情緒升高。此次選舉終結
共和黨自 2024 年總統大選以來同時掌控白宮與國會兩院的局面，美國再度形成
「分裂政府」(Divided Government)格局。未來川普政府推動重要法案、預算案、
人事任命及稅制改革等政策，將面臨更大的國會制衡與協商壓力，民主黨亦可能
加強對行政部門的監督與調查。就經濟層面而言，市場預期大規模減稅、財政擴
張及部分貿易政策推動速度可能放緩，有助於降低財政赤字進一步擴大的風險；
惟兩黨對立加深 ， 亦可能提高預算協商 、 債務上限及政府融資等議題的不確定性 。 
美國民主黨於 2026 年 11 月期中選舉取得國會參、眾兩院控制權後，未來人
工智慧監管政策走向受到關注。其中，由無黨籍參議員桑德斯（ Bernie Sanders ）
及民主黨眾議員卡薩爾（Greg Casar ）於 9 月分別提出的《2026 年禁止人工超級
智慧法案》 （Ban Artificial Superintelligence Act of 2026） ，主張禁止開發、部署、
持有及資助具備超越人類認知能力或可能威脅人類安全的人工超級智慧 （ASI），
並設立內閣層級的聯邦「人工智慧部」（Department of Artif icial Intelligence），
統籌先進 AI 系統的安全審查及監管。此外，法案規劃在監管機構完成籌設並建
立相關安全規範前 ， 暫停訓練運算量達10²⁵次以上的先進 AI 系統進行訓練 、 修改
及微調，並限制未發布系統的部署。截至 11 月 8 日，該法案仍處於國會委員會
審議階段，尚未正式立法。隨著民主黨將於 2027 年 1 月取得國會主導權，後續
法案審議進展及其對美國 AI 技術發展、企業投資與國際競爭的影響，值得持續
關注。

二、歐元區—通膨壓力持續增強 
 歐元區 10 月 HICP 通膨上升 0.2 個百分點至 4%， 核心通膨(不含食品及能源)
則持平為 2.5%。該月食品及能源通膨皆持續上揚，反映國際原油價格居高不下，
並透過肥料、物流成本上漲，間接推升食品價格。另外，服務通膨上升 0.1 個百
分點至 3.3%， 部分反映交通費用上漲的影響 ， 惟服務通膨仍是低於今年上半年的
平均水準。展望未來，近期布蘭特原油雖回落至 100 美元內，但美伊談判陷入僵
局 ， 仍預期年底前布蘭特原油位於每桶90 美元以上 ， 將對通膨帶來額外壓力， 後
續須關注能源漲價可能會透過運輸、物流等成本管道傳導至核心商品及服務價格，
進而提高核心通膨黏性。 
 其他經濟數據包含：(1)歐元區 9 月失業率上升 0.1 個百分點為 6.5%，失業人
數增加 12.2 萬人，主要是法國失業人數增加較多所致，係因大規模抗議活動的影
響；(2)歐元區 9 月零售額月減 0.7%、第三季季減 0.5%，物價上漲帶來的負面影
響逐步顯現，預期第四季零售額將延續此一疲態。 
三、日本—薪資成長動能增強，春鬥效應持續擴散 
 9 月薪資年增 4.6%，創 1997 年以來最大增幅，除受夏季獎金大增 32.6%支撐
外，經常性薪資亦年增 3.7%， 顯示今年春鬥的加薪成果正逐步由大型企業擴散至
中小企業。隨企業獲利保持強勁，加上日本最大工會聯合組織 （Rengo） 已表態明
年將爭取更高加薪幅度，預期薪資成長動能仍可延續，有助支撐民間消費，但同
時也可能推升服務價格，增加通膨持續性。 
四、中國—關注 11 中旬川習會發展 
10 月官方製造業 PMI 下降 0.8 點至 49.3，主因十一長假前需求提前釋放，以
及外部環境不確定性升高所致。新訂單、生產指數分別降至 49.4、49.8，新出口
訂單亦降至 48.7。此外，生產及出廠價格指數分別降至56.8 及 51.7，反映成本與
售價漲幅已有收斂跡象；惟就業指數則持平於 48.4，顯示就業仍顯疲弱。不過，
RatingDog 製造業 PMI 略增 0.1 點至 52.1，反映 RatingDog 調查以中小型出口企
業為主，在全球 AI 相關需求的支持下，仍能保持穩定的擴張表現；惟RatingDog
製造業產出物價指數連續兩個月下調，投入物價指數則持續上揚，反映競爭環境
激烈，迫使廠商犧牲利潤以降價求售。

8

官方非製造業 PMI 下降 0.5 點至 49.7， 其中建築業商務活動指數由50.3 降至
48.0，服務業商務活動指數則小幅下降至 50，顯示十一長假消費動能並不特別活
躍 ； 非製造業就業指數雖微升至46.0， 但仍遠低於榮枯線 ， 不利於後續內需復甦 。
另外，RatingDog 服務業指數下降 0.8 點至 50.8，亦反映需求增速減弱，使得
RatingDog 綜合產出指數為 52，較 9 月的 52.4 回落，為近三個月最慢，製造業產
出增快為能抵銷服務業擴張放緩。 
展望未來 ， 中國製造業仍面臨外需不確定 、 內需復甦偏弱及價格競爭激烈等 ，
惟全球 AI 產業需求可望擴張，加上人行上調外匯存款準備率，有助緩和人民幣
升值對出口競爭力的壓力 ， 接有助於繼續支持出口需求 。 此外 ， 若11 月 APEC 期
間川習會釋出改善中美經貿關係訊號，將有助提振企業信心及出口前景，惟後續
仍須觀察全球需求及地緣政治發展。 
五、印度及東南亞—馬來西亞央行按兵不動；印尼、越南出口亮眼；泰國通膨壓
力上升 
(1) 印尼：9 月出口額達 275.9 億美元，創四年新高、年增 11.87%，非油氣出口較
去年成長 12.39%，以動植物油脂成長 89.04%為最，礦物燃料及電氣機械及設
備分別成長 12%、24.37%，抵銷石油和天然氣出口下滑 1%的影響，對美國、
中國出口分別成長 23.21%、11.82%。9 月進口額達 242.3 億美元、年增 18.41%，
增幅創 6 個月新低，其中成品油進口成長 37%，非石油和天然氣進口額年增
17.59%，以電氣機械設備(25.9%)及塑膠製品(16.3%)增幅較大，自中國進口額
大增 30.23%、自東協和歐盟進口分別成長 20.98%、18.21%。總計 9 月，貿易
順差為 33.6 億美元，2026 年前 9 個月，貿易順差 106.1 億美元，出口和進口
分別成長 5.58%和 19.67%。 
(2) 泰國：10 月消費者物價年增率(CPI)從 9 月的 2.82%飆升至 3.9%，3 年多來首
次突破泰國央行 1%-3%的目標區間，主要受油價上漲和洪水導致食品價格上
漲的影響，泰國商務部預計 2026 年整體通膨率平均在 1.8%-2.2%之間，但由
於基期較低，物價壓力可能在年底前持續走高。雖然通膨壓力顯著增強，但官
方評估此波物價飆漲應屬暫時現象。 
(3) 馬來西亞：馬來西亞央行(BNM)11 月會議將隔夜政策利率 （OPR） 維持在2.75%
不變，為連續第八次維持利率不變，符合市場預期。政策聲明強化對經濟成長

### Page 10

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

9

的樂觀評估，表示在強勁的出口表現和持續的內需支撐下，2026 年全年經濟
成長率有望突破 5%，預計 2027 年經濟活動在科技出口帶動下將保持韌性。
通膨方面 ， 央行指出地緣政治發展 、 大宗商品價格上漲及不利天氣狀況將對通
膨構成上行壓力，但現有的燃油和公用事業補貼應有助於抑制通膨 。整體而言 ，
央行目前政策立場仍支持物價穩定且可持續的經濟成長， 並樂觀看待未來經濟
及通膨前景的發展。 
(4) 越南: 10 月出口額年增 51.4%，達 646.7 億美元的歷史新高，其中電子產品出
口年增 102.6%為主要成長動力，機械設備出口年增 49.8%居次。同時，10 月
進口額達 573.6 億美元、年增幅略放緩至 43.1%，其中，電子產品進口成長
84.5%，能源進口則因油價上揚而大幅增加 74.1%。總計 10 月，貿易順差為
73.1 億美元，連續第二個月轉為順差；2026 年前 10 月，貿易逆差小幅收斂至
120.7 億美元，出口額成長放緩至 27.5%、進口額成長率為 37.4%。10 月 CPI
年通膨率小幅上升至 5.09%，創 5 月以來最高，其中，以交通運輸費用上漲
15.57%為最，酒精飲料和菸草 （4.40%） 、住房和公用事業 （6.50%） 及醫療保
健（1.75%）價格漲幅均有所加快，由於通膨已連三月突破央行目標區間，將
加大升息的壓力。 
六、台灣—通膨漲幅收斂，尚無輸入性通膨疑慮 
10 月消費者物價指數 （CPI） 年增2.33%，較 9 月的 2.74%明顯回落，顯示整
體通膨壓力已有降溫跡象，但仍近半年皆高於 2%。10 月物價漲幅收斂，主要受
蔬菜價格轉跌、電價漲幅縮小，以及部分商品與服務價格漲勢趨緩等因素影響 ，
惟外食費年增 3.01%， 已連續5 個月超過 3%， 民眾對物價上漲的感受仍較明顯 。
另，10 月以新台幣計價的進口物價年增 33.32%，創近 50 年最大漲幅，主要受國
際原油及 AI 需求推升電子零組件價格所致，惟相關電子零組件多供出口使用，
對國內消費端影響有限，加以政府採取油價平穩措施，因此主計總處認為目前並
無輸入性通膨問題 。展望未來，隨中秋節比較基期因素消退 ，主計總處預估11 月
CPI 年增率仍將高於 2%，且漲幅可能較 10 月擴大。

2026 年 7-9 月期 GDP 予測(最終版) 
～前期比年率▲0.4％の小幅マイナス成長を予想～ 
経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト 新家義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

11 月16 日に公表される26 年７-９月期の実質
ＧＤＰ成長率を前期比年率▲0.4％（前期比▲
0.1％）と予測する。10 月 30 日の段階では前期
比年率＋0.4％（前期比＋0.1％）と小幅プラス
成長を予想していたが、その後公表された経済
指標の結果を反映し、小幅マイナス成長へと予
測値を下方修正する。 
本日公表された26 年９月分の国際収支統計の
結果を反映し、26 年７-９月期の実質輸出を前期
比＋0.1％（従来予測値：同＋1.2％）へ下方修
正する。筆者の９月分の想定と比べてサービス
輸出が大きく下振れたことが主因である。実質
輸入も前期比＋3.0％（従来予測値：同＋
3.3％）と下方修正するが、輸出の下方修正度合
いの方が大きいことから、外需の予測値を前期
比年率寄与度▲1.9％Pt（従来予測値：同▲
1.3％Pt）へ下方修正している。 
また、９月分の家計調査と家計消費状況調査
が想定よりも下振れたことを反映し、個人消費
の予測値も前期比▲0.3％と、従来予測値の同▲
0.2％から小幅下方修正した。

物価高による下押しや耐久財需要の反動に加え、天候不順の影響もあって個人消費が減少する
ことが成長率を押し下げる見込みである。特に、８～９月には長雨・大雨や台風などの天候不順
が相次ぎ、外出手控えや旅行・宿泊の見送りなどを通じて消費が下押しされたとみられる。一
方、設備投資は高い伸びとなり、景気を下支えするとみられる。設備投資の伸びには特殊要因も
（％）

2/2
一部含まれているが、それを除いても堅調だったと考えられる。
７-９月期は４四半期ぶりのマイナス成長になると予想するが、マイナス幅は大きくない。今回
のマイナス成長には天候不順という一時的な下押し要因も含まれており、過去３四半期に高い成
長が続いた後であることや、企業部門の底堅さが維持されていることを踏まえれば、景気回復の
流れが途切れたと判断する必要はないだろう。
物価高による節約志向は根強く、個人消費の回復にはなお時間を要するとみられるが、設備投
資を中心に企業部門が好調さを維持している点は好材料である。家計部門が力強さを欠く一方、
企業部門が景気を下支えする構図のもと、日本経済は均してみれば緩やかな回復基調にあると判
断している。

Inventec (2356.TW)

Bull/Bear: Inventec (2356.TW)

### Page 6

Inventec (2356.TW)
10 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Inventec
Company description
Established in 1975 and listed in 1996, Taiwan-based Inventec is a key ODM
of NBs, servers, smartphones, and wearable/Io T devices to global customers.
Major shareholders include the founder Mr. Yeh Kuo-I, whose family has (both
directly and indirectly) a c35% stake.
Investment strategy
We rate Inventec shares as Buy. We expect stable demand in NB and server to
help support near-term earnings, while product diversification within smart

devices to areas such as Io T and medical space should help improve L-T
margins. We expect the company's solid cash yield to serve as a downside
cushion.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$65 for Inventec shares is based on 17x 2027 EPS, which
is slightly above the mid-point of its historical trading range, reflecting the
company's product diversification and stable demand outlook led by servers
and commercial NBs, as well as increasing AI server exposure that could

potentially drive margin expansion and better earnings momentum.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) Slower-than-expected shipment growth/weaker-than-expected
sell-through in HomePods and AirPods could lead to downside in our sales
forecasts; 2) If competitors adopt aggressive pricing strategies to gain market
shares, our ASP and margin assumptions could face downside risk; 3) Lower-
than-expected server margins driven by rising exposure to Chinese customers

could impact our earnings forecasts negatively; 4) Deterioration in NB
demand and investment in solar would hurt our forecasts; and 5) Stronger-
than-expected Taiwan dollar could lead to FX loss and downside to our
current forecasts.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

### Page 9

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

ab 10 November 2026
Global Research
First Read
ASMedia Technology Inc
Inventory adjustment weighs on N-T outlook
Q326 earnings broadly in line; PC demand remains weak
ASMedia reported Q326 EPS of NT$26.39, broadly in line with UBSe but above Street,
helped by ~NT$1.5bn of non-op income mainly from WT Micro. Q326 sales of NT
$2.66bn declined 2% QoQ and 33% YoY, reflecting continued weakness in PC demand
amid elevated memory prices and the shift at its major customer from AM5 back to the

lower-cost AM4 platform. Q326 GM came in at 51.6% (+1.3ppt QoQ), helped by a
more favorable product mix and pricing adjustments.  Opex increased to NT$795mn on
higher R&D spending including 12nm chip tape-out and a 10-15% increase in
headcount expansion, resulting in  OpM declining 2.1ppt QoQ to 21.7%.  Q326 sales mix
was 74% for host controllers, ~21% automotive and ~5%  device.
Sell-through improving, but  inventory adjustment to weigh through Q127F

ASMedia is seeing improving PC motherboard demand, with Q326 motherboard
production up >20% QoQ and management viewing H126 as the near-term trough.
However, elevated memory prices have driven AMD's product mix back toward the
lower-ASP AM4 platform (AM4  >70% of Q326 chipset shipments up from >50% in
Q226), while AMD continues to digest inventory. It noted AMD's sales contribution has
already declined from >50% of sales in H126 to <50% in Q326, and management

expects the inventory correction to continue through Q127, delaying the benefit from
improving motherboard sell-through. We expect October to mark the trough (monthly
sales down 43% MoM) and will remain muted on AMD's inventory control, resulting  in
Q426 sales  declining~33% QoQ  while GM should decline by 1.6ppt QoQ to 50.0% as
slower pull in of AM5 could lead to inventory provision, despite a reversal in H127E.
2027E recovery more back-end-loaded

ASMedia remains confident in retaining its sole-supplier position for AMD’s next-
generation Olympic (AM5.5) chipset platform, with contribution expected from H227
and ASP ~50-60% higher than AM4. We expect USB4 demand to recover alongside the
Olympic ramp, with additional upside from a new US robotics customer from H227.
Separately, we expect AM5 shipments to recover  and potential  inventory writebacks to
provide upside to GM. We believe PCIe packet-switch contribution could be more

gradual given slower design-in progress. Overall, we estimate ASMedia’s 2027E sales to
grow ~5% YoY (vs. previous 17% YoY expectations), with growth skewed to H227.
Valuation: Retain Buy with PT of NT$1,500
We lower our 2026/27/28E EPS by  2%/1%/2% primarily on softer PC demand and
inventory adjustment, offset by higher better earnings contribution from WT Micro. We
cut our PT to NT$1,500 (from NT$1,700), based on our SOTP valuation  (18x for core

earnings and 11.5x for non-core), implying 13x 2027E PE.  Maintain Buy rating as stock is
trading near trough 14x NTM PE  vs an 11-48x historical range, while its diversification
into non-PC   should support its topline/OP to recover from 2027E.

Company Description
ASMedia is a Taiwan-based fabless IC design house founded by parent company Asustek
Computer Inc. in 2004. ASMedia is dedicated to high-speed input/output (I/O) controllers,
such as USB, PCIe and SATA, and its products are mainly used in desktop PCs, motherboards

and add-on cards. In 2024, ASMedia generated 84% of its revenue from host controllers and
16% from device controllers. The company collaborates with Advanced Micro Devices (AMD)
to design southbridge chipsets for Zen-based desktop central processing units (CPUs). AMD is
the company's largest client account, contributing 58% of revenue in 2024.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We base our price target on sum-of-the-parts methodology. We value ASMedia's core and

non-core businesses based on a target PE multiple.
Fabless integrated circuit (IC) design houses face a variety of uncertainty, including rapidly
changing technology, end-market demand, competition, employee turnover and foundry
wafer costs. They are also vulnerable to legal disputes over intellectual property rights.
We believe risks for ASMedia include: 1) AMD's chipset insourcing or other design houses
entering AMD's chipset business; 2) faster penetration of USB4 native support in chipsets; 3)

more entrants in the USB device controller market; 4) continued suspension of China tender
projects; and 5) increased US export controls impacting China's computing capability.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 2

Earnings revision 
We revise down our 2026 earnings estimates by 8% to factor in 3Q26 results (we revise 
up our 2026E revenue estimates by 1%). We revise down 2027/28E earnings estimates 
by 9%/8% to reflect product mix with higher contribution from the server business (we 
revise up our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 6/6%). We now expect the GM expansion 
to be slower due to the higher server business contribution that is margin dilutive, thus 
revise down GPM by 0.2/0.2/0.2ppt in 2026/27/28E.

Valuation:  
We reiterate our Neutral rating on Inventec with a new 12m TP of NT$56 (from NT$61), 
based on 14.2x 2027E P/E (unchanged), which is the company’s average P/E for the past 
10 years, and factoring in our earnings revision.

### Page 3

Investment thesis and Valuation & Risks

Inventec is an ODM company focusing on the design and manufacturing of PCs, servers, 
and consumer electronics. While we like Inventec’s solid position in the AI server ODM 
market, we are Neutral rated as we believe valuation already reflects near-term 
positives. We model Inventec earnings growth in 2026-28E to be driven by the 
commercial PC demand recovery and AI server ramp-up. We model AI servers to 
contribute more in 2026-28E. We see P/E valuation as fair at the current level compared 
to its peers and already reflects the solid growth in 2026-28E. Catalysts for further 
upside/downside would be its customers’ demand on AI servers and general server 
demand outlook.

Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$56 is based on 14.2x 2027E P/E, which is 
the company’s average P/E for the past 10 years. 
Key upside/downside risks: (1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
(2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and (3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

Company Brief 
유틸리티/건설 
Analyst 문경원, CFA 
kyeongwon.moon@meritz.co.kr 
RA 이동석 
dongseok.lee@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 Review: 바닥을 잡는다면 지금  
믹스 개선에 따른 이익률 개선 조금씩 관찰 중 
3Q26 연결 영업이익은 2,612억원(+90.6% YoY) 을 기록하며 컨센서스(2,455억원)
를 소폭 상회했다. 밥캣(1,674억원)이 컨센서스(1,761억원)를 소폭 하회한 가운데, 
에너빌리티 부문 영업이익(+179% YoY, OPM 6.6%) 이 호실적을 이끌었다. 환율 하
락에도 불구, 가스터빈, 원전 등 고수익성 프로젝트의 비중 상승이 주요 원인이다. 
3Q26 누적 수주는 10.0조원(+85.5% YoY) 을 기록했다. 4Q26 가스복합 EPC 수주 
등을 감안하면 연간 가이던스(13.3조원) 달성은 무난하다. 한수원과의 협상 결렬로 
이목이 집중된 체코 두코바니 시공 수주는 2027년 인식될 확률이 높아 보인다. 주간
사가 바뀌더라도 이미 프로젝트에 참여 중인 동사를 배제하기는 쉽지 않다.

대미투자 이후, 연말~연초 수주 모멘텀에 집중 
연말 연초에 다음과 수주 모멘텀이 집중되면서, 주가 상승을 이끌 전망이다. 
1) 대형원전: 정부 자금 중심의 Westinghouse LLI(장납기 기자재) 발주가 몰린다. 
우선 미국 DOE(에너지부)가 제공한 대출을 기반으로 미국 수주가 기대된다.  폴란드 
정부 역시 최근 LLI 발주 작업을 시작했다. 한편, 한국 정부는 연말까지 100억 달러 
규모의 대미 투자 송금을 협의 중인데, 이 역시 DOC(상무부) 주도의 대형 원전 건설
을 위한 LLI 발주에 쓰일 가능성이 높다 
2) SMR: 오래 기다려왔던 Nuscale 향 수주가 시작되고 있다. 우선 29일 모듈 1기 
제작 계약을 체결했다. 현금이 부족한 Nuscale이 용기를 냈다는 건, 곧 Nuscale-
TVA 간 PPA 가 임박했음을 의미한다. 본격적인 수주는 PPA 직후 예상된다.  
3) 가스 터빈: 2026년 초 해외 수주(7기)는 2029년까지 인도 계획이다. 이후 2030
년 슬랏 예약을 완료하고 2031년 물량을 논의 중으로 보인다. 지금은 2030년 슬랏
에 대한 PO가 나와 수주잔고에 인식할 타이밍이다. 역시 수출 물량으로 추측한다.

성장에 대한 기대가 사라진 밸류에이션, 지금이 바닥 
투자의견 Buy를 유지, 할인율 상승을 반영하여 적정주가를 109,000원으로 -17% 
하향 조정한다. 금리 상승, 환율 하락, 트럼프 지지율 하락 등 Top-down 리스크에 
최근 조정을 받았지만, 추가적인 주가 하락 폭은 제한적일 전망이다. EV/선행 수주잔
고 지표는 현재 1.0배 수준까지 하락하며, 원전의 성장성이 주목받지 못했던 
2021~2024년 수준에 근접하게 내려왔다.  
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 30

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 109,000원

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
악조건 속에서도 이 정도 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 영업실적은 매출액 5조 7,536억원(yoy +30.2%, qoq 
`-9.1%), 영업이익 8,992억원(yoy +61.3%, qoq -13.5%)으로 당사 추정치에 대체로 
부합하였다. 조업일수 감소와 원화 강세로 매출액이 감소했으며, 임단협 타결로 
인한 성과금 및 격려금이 당분기에 비용으로 반영되었다. 해양플랜트 부문에서는 
RUYA 프로젝트의 공사 진행률 확대와 TRION 프로젝트의 C/O(계약 변경) 
180억원이 추가로 반영되며 매출액이 전분기대비 0.7% 증가하였다. 엔진기계 
부문은 환율 하락을 판가 상승과 D/F(이중연료) 비중 증가로 상쇄하며 전분기와 
유사한 영업실적을 달성했다. 
2027년에도 지속될 수주 시점 효과 
3분기 대형선 야드의 수주 시점별 매출 비중은 2022년 4%, 2023년 45%, 2024년 
31%, 2025년 20% 수준으로 파악된다. 상대적으로 선가가 낮았던 2022~2023년 
물량이 여전히 매출의 절반 가량을 차지하고 있는 셈이다. 따라서 2027년 말까지도 
고선가 물량의 매출 비중 확대가 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이는 LNG운반선 매출 
비중 감소에도 불구하고 수익성을 방어할 수 있는 핵심 요인으로 작용할 전망이다.   
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 550,000원으로 하향 
투자의견은 매수로 유지하나, 목표주가는 550,000원으로 34.5% 하향한다. 환율 
가정을 기존 1,450원 수준에서 1,400원으로 조정함에 따라 목표주가 산정의 기준이 
되는 2027~2028년 실적추정치를 하향조정하였다. 또한 미국 네오 클라우드 
업체들의 자금 조달 여건 악화로 데이터센터 증설이 지연되고 있어, 발전용 엔진 
증설을 근거로 동사에 부여하였던 프리미엄을 제거하여 P/E 배수를 19.8배로 
하향조정했다. 그러나 본업인 상선 부문은 향후 고부가가치 가스선의 발주 강세를 
전망하기 때문에 투자의견은 매수로 유지한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일

TUẦN 02-06/11 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -16.26 điểm tương ứng -0.93%, với thanh 
khoản mở rộng

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
Diễn biến phân hóa và bi ến động mạnh có thể còn tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ vọng sẽ tiếp tục 
hướng lên thử thách lại các vùng cản phía trên

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -16.26 điểm tương ứng -0.93%, với thanh khoản mở 
rộng. Thiết bị dụng cụ Y tế, Tiện ích khí và Bảo hiểm là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt 
nhất trong tuần qua. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị 
-870 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã MBB, VIC và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
ở mức 504 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng -2,010 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua 
và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -10,888 hợp đồng. Tự doanh duy trì mua ròng 1,208 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -4,070 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam có tuần giảm điểm thứ 2 liên tiếp khi VN-Index lùi về kiểm định vùng hỗ trợ 
quanh 1.725 điểm, với phiên cuối tuần có thời điểm giảm hơn 30 điểm trước khi thu hẹp 
đà giảm nhờ lực cầu ở nhóm chứng khoán. Áp lực bán tập trung ở nhóm vốn hóa lớn, đặc 
biệt là bộ đôi Vingroup (VIC, VHM) cùng một số cổ phiếu ngân hàng và công nghệ, khiến 
chỉ số thiếu lực đỡ. Giao dịch của khối ngoại diễn biến trái chiều và giằng co — có phiên 
mua ròng trở lại ở một số mã, nhưng vẫn xen kẽ bán ròng ở nhóm trụ như VHM, VCB, cho 
thấy dòng vốn ngoại chưa xác lập xu hướng rõ ràng. Bối cảnh bên ngoài kém thuận lợi khi 
nhóm cổ phiếu công nghệ tại Mỹ bị bán tháo, kéo theo các chỉ số chính toàn cầu đồng 
loạt lao dốc. Mặc dù vậy, kỳ vọng vào động thái mở kênh hoán đổi ngoại tệ của NHNN để 
hỗ trợ thanh khoản, hạ lãi suất đã giúp tâm lý nhà đầu tư trong nước ổn định và không 
xuất hiện áp lực bán tháo.

Chỉ số VNINDEX

Source: Trading View, KB Securities Vietnam

Ngắn hạn 
(Dưới 3 tháng)

Trung hạn (Từ 3 đến 9 tháng)

Chỉ báo  Nhận định xu hướng Hành động 
Xanh đậm  Khả năng tăng điểm cao Mua mạnh 
Xanh nhạt  Khả năng tăng điểm vừa phải Mua từng phần

Đỏ  Khả năng giảm điểm cao Bán mạnh

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tuần qua hình thành mẫu nến spinning thân nhỏ với bóng trên/dưới khá dài, 
cùng thanh khoản gia tăng, thể hiện sự giằng co mạnh nhưng về tổng thể lại cho thấy diễn 
biến đã cân bằng trở lại sau tuần giảm điểm trước đó. Trên khung ngày, mặc dù trải qua 
các phiên biến động tăng/giảm mạnh đan xen nhưng việc cho phản ứng bật tăng tích cực 
tại quanh đường biên dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn cho thấy tương quan 
vẫn đang nghiêng về chiều hướng tăng. Diễn biến phân hóa và biến động mạnh có thể còn 
tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ tiếp tục hướng lên thử thách lại các vùng cản phía trên.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1800 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1750 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1700 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1660 (+-15)

### Page 4

4

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Document 373: G10 FX Outlook 2027_ It’s hard to look beyond the Fed

Source PDF: all report pdf/G10 FX Outlook 2027_ It’s hard to look beyond the Fed.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 17
Status: ok

### Page 1

In a very uncertain world, this year’s more hawkish steer from
the Federal Reserve has provided a constant and supportive
anchor for the dollar. While the immediate picture for the dollar
looks quite constructive, a turn in the inﬂation cycle by the
second half of 2027 should usher in a modest downtrend

Chris Turner and Francesco Pesole
Executive summary
We all know that the ‘big dollar’ call sets the tone for global
markets – and that call is largely inﬂuenced by the Fed. What
seems clear as we go to press is that the central bank is not about
to turn dovish. In fact, inﬂuential centrist Christopher Waller has just
made his case for opening the door to a 50bp hike this December.
That hawkish, dollar-positive tilt stands in contrast to the typically
negative seasonal factors faced by the dollar in December. This
year, however, the dollar has enough positives to defy that pattern.
US growth might slow slightly from the 3.4% quarter-on-quarter
annualised rate in the third quarter but remains strong. And what

### Page 2

seems to be a strengthening jobs market and inﬂation way above
target look set to keep tightening expectations ﬁrm for a couple of
months yet. That probably means the low-yielding yen and Swiss
franc can remain on the back foot over the coming months.
It seems a diﬃcult call to make today, but the ING house view
forecasts just one 25bp Fed hike in December to conclude this
recalibration of Fed policy. We are not forecasting an AI bust but are
positioning ourselves at the dovish end of the Fed spectrum for

2027. Here, further pressure on the housing market, alongside
lower energy prices and softer wages should give the Fed enough
room to ease restrictive policy in the ﬁnal months of 2027. Such an
outcome feels a distant prospect right now.
An ‘up then down’ dollar baseline view points to relatively conﬁned
ranges in 2027. But in keeping with the theme of this outlook, ‘Fat
tails, slim ranges’, extreme scenarios need to be considered. We
look at those risks in more detail in other articles in this publication.

Beyond the reaction functions of the Fed and the European Central
Bank, the two most important drivers of EUR/USD will likely be the
path of the AI story and the geopolitical/energy scenario.
We look at the range of possible EUR/USD outcomes in light of those
risks below. As we learned last year, pushing a baseline view too
heavily without suﬃcient regard to foreign policy possibilities can
prove dangerous.

Currency sections
EUR/USD: Hope for the best, prepare for the
worst
Spot Year ahead bias 4Q26 1Q27 2Q27 3Q27 4Q27
EUR/USD 1.11 Bullish 1.13 1.13 1.16 1.17 1.18
Fed stays hawkish through the ﬁrst quarter of 2027: Underpinning
dollar strength through the second half of this year has been the
hawkish Federal Reserve. New Chair Kevin Warsh’s commitment to

restore monetary credibility has put paid to dollar debasement fears.
We look for hawkish Fed pricing to remain the dominant dollar theme
through the ﬁrst quarter of 2027, driven by a solid US labour market,
inﬂation still in the 3.5-4.0% area and AI investment still contributing to
growth. Our baseline sees the dollar turning lower in the second
quarter, when inﬂation will be falling back to 2.0% and money markets
will be questioning whether the Fed needs to keep policy at 100bp

above neutral with inﬂation back at target. This assumes one Fed hike
this December to 4.25% and that the Fed is able to cut rates at the end
of 2027.
ECB to deliver ﬁnal hike in December: The eurozone economy has put
in a resilient performance this year despite higher energy prices. The
European Central Bank (ECB) has already hiked rates twice this year and
is expected to hike to 2.75% in December. That should be enough to
contain inﬂation, which is still largely driven by energy. Second-round

eﬀects are nowhere to be seen. Our team sees eurozone growth slowly
Your privacy is important

You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.
Essential

Targeting Cookies

### Page 4

picking up through 2027 to deliver full-year growth of 1.2%. Like the
Fed, we think the ECB will have room to ease restrictive monetary
policy towards the end of 2027. There will also be a focus on who
succeeds ECB President Christine Lagarde by next February. Klaas Knot
and Pablo Hernández de Cos have made it to the ﬁnal round. A sea
change in ECB policy looks unlikely, given that a new chief economist
will balance whoever gets the top job.
EUR/USD baseline and risks: Speculators are well on their way to

building extensive short euro positions. The data suggests the European
buy-side, just as was the case in early 2025, is prepared to hold
positions in US assets with very low FX hedge ratios. Our base case
assumes continued trading in a 1.10-1.15 range over the next four to
ﬁve months and then a modest rebound towards 1.18 by end-2027 as
US inﬂation drops and the Fed loosens restrictive policy. At a time of a
resilient US economy, a lower EUR/USD is probably the more vulnerable

side over the coming months. This could potentially be driven by a
large spike in energy prices or the French budgetary situation curtailing
any further ECB hikes and, in an extreme case, triggering ECB bond
market intervention. The most pressing upside EUR/USD risk is probably
a much deeper stock market correction, with US consumers hit by
wealth eﬀects and the market swinging towards Fed cuts, not hikes.
USD/JPY: Friends in high places
Spot Year ahead bias 4Q26 1Q27 2Q27 3Q

USD/JPY 160.00 Bearish 158.00 158.00 155.00 15
160 remains the line in the sand: Ever since Washington joined Tokyo’s
defence of the yen in late July, USD/JPY has not been able to hold a
move above 160. The ﬁrst joint intervention to support the yen since
1998 sent a strong signal of aligned interests. Japan does not want a
weaker yen to make the cost of living any worse. Washington does not
want a higher USD/JPY to drag the USD/Asia complex with it,

undermining eﬀorts to level the playing ﬁeld for US manufacturers.
Even though the US Treasury only intervened in small amounts of
EUR/JPY at the time, the commitment to keep USD/JPY under 160 looks
clear. A hawkish Fed makes that task harder, but the Bank of Japan (BoJ)
should be closing the rate gap. We look for a hike to 1.50% in December
and then another in March. A pronounced turn lower in USD/JPY may
not emerge until the second quarter of next year, however, when we

have short-dated US rates ﬁnally starting to drop.

### Page 5

Takaichi tries to reﬂate the economy: Prime Minister Sanae Takaichi’s
policies are focused on building strength both domestically and
internationally. Domestically, her party, the Liberal Democratic Party
(LDP), is pursuing a strategic JPY370tr investment target by 2040,
utilising both public and private money, targeted across 17 strategic
sectors. Growing at around 0.8%, the Japanese economy is already
around trend output, and Japan may be one of the few G10 economies

with a positive output gap in 2027. This means the BoJ could be one of
the central banks to hike more than expected or be among the last to
cut rates in a global downtrend. This backdrop is keeping the BoJ
hawkish and the focus on whether corporate proﬁts are still driving
wages higher. Recent evidence suggests they are, which is one of the
reasons why the BoJ has recently concluded that its 2% inﬂation target
has been stably met. A key focus in 2027 will be whether Japan can use

productive investment to oﬀset its labour shortage challenges.
Yen remains cheap: Driven by years of low inﬂation and nominal
depreciation, the real trade-weighted yen remains exceptionally weak.
We estimate that USD/JPY is around 20% overvalued on medium-term
fundamentals, with further yen downside now protected by the Tokyo-
Washington alliance. Our call for 152 next year is largely based on the
dollar decline from spring next year. The surprise package, however,

could be Japan’s domestic story, were the economy to start beneﬁting
more broadly from the AI buildout. Here, Japan’s role is supplying the
hardware, manufacturing technology and infrastructure. We have seen
in Korea this year how the AI export story has broadened into stronger
domestic demand and a more hawkish central bank. Higher growth
rates domestically could be an important piece of the jigsaw that
ﬁnally brings home a more substantial part of Japanese earnings

retained overseas.
GBP/USD: Another year of tight ranges
Spot Year ahead bias 4Q26 1Q27 2Q27 3Q27 4
GBP/USD 1.33 Mildly Bullish 1.36 1.36 1.38 1.38 1
BoE matches Fed hawkishness: One abiding feature of core money
markets this year was how readily traders were prepared to price in
Bank of England (BoE) hikes. Sterling rates did have one of the highest
correlations with oil prices, but even when Brent corrected below
$100/bbl recently, markets still held onto views of a terminal BoE rate

of near 4.75%. Important for the GBP/USD story is the timing of the
relative US and UK inﬂation trajectories in 2027. Our team sees US
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.
You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.
Essential
Performance Cookies
Targeting Cookies

### Page 6

inﬂation coming back towards 2% in the second quarter as energy base
eﬀects drop out. In the UK, however, the lagged implementation of the
energy price caps means that inﬂation does not start to turn lower until
the third quarter. Our call is therefore that US rates could turn lower
before those in the UK, and that GBP/USD could be retesting the 1.37-
1.38 range highs in the second quarter.
Some creeping optimism: It might not seem like it given that GBP/USD

is still trapped in the middle of its current 1.32-1.37 range, but investors
are getting more positive on the pound. The price of the one-year 25-
delta risk reversal, which measures the price of a call option over a put
option, is now showing the smallest skew towards GBP puts since March.
That has not shown up in the FX positioning data yet, where asset
managers are still running large sterling shorts by historical standards.
Buy-side surveys also show international fund managers are still

substantially underweight in UK equities. But positioning does present
an upside risk to GBP/USD as we head into year-end. If it were to break
higher, we suspect it might be driven more by surprise weakness in the
dollar, however. This is because the BoE tightening cycle into next year
looks fully priced.
Fiscal beneﬁts: In a world where ﬁscal trajectories are being
increasingly scrutinised, the UK falls on the more encouraging side of
the ledger. At least the direction of travel looks encouraging for the

budget deﬁcit, while the fact that the UK has a ﬁscal rule, unlike the US,
is welcome. We also note that the UK has quite a healthy duration on
its government debt stock at 13.5 years, compared with 5.9 years for
the US. The UK government’s reﬁnancing requirements as a percentage
of GDP are much smaller at 8.7%, according to the IMF, compared with
30.8% for the US. Whether sterling would outperform the dollar in a
major bond market sell-oﬀ remains to be seen, but the UK’s debt

maturity proﬁle and near-term debt ﬁnancing needs certainly look
better than those in the US.
EUR/JPY: Steady, with downside risks
Spot Year ahead bias 4Q26 1Q27 2Q27 3Q
EUR/JPY 177.00 Neutral 179.00 179.00 180.00 17
Positive correlation with growth and equities: Both Europe and Japan
have faced their struggles with high energy prices, but both economies
have performed quite well. Europe has beneﬁted from the growing
ﬁscal stimulus put in place last year, which is starting to show through

Your privacy is important

### Page 7

in series like German manufacturing orders. Japan’s economy has been
protected by the government’s use of energy stockpiles, and in 2027,
Japanese consumers will beneﬁt from a large cut in VAT on food next
April. Yet EUR/JPY remains positively correlated with growth and pro-risk
sentiment in general and would suﬀer in an equity downturn. Decent
advances in corporate proﬁts suggest that a large equity correction is
not a given, while forecasts for continued growth and a turn in inﬂation

in the second half of 2027 make the case for continued equity gains
and a supported EUR/JPY.
Focus on France: Japanese investors have historically been big
investors in French sovereign bond markets, although these ﬂows seem
to have stalled over recent years. French debt had been seen as a
higher-yielding, liquid alternative to German Bunds. However, France’s
unresolved budgetary situation and the threat of further sovereign
downgrades urge caution. Next up is the S&P rating review on 27

November. Our base case is that the current French government passes
a 2027 budget by decree and that some modest ﬁscal consolidation is
enough to avoid dramatic downgrades. This is no 2011-2012 situation,
when the market was shut to Greek sovereign issuance. Were we to be
underestimating the pressure on French debt, the solution would be
euro-negative, especially if the ECB needed to use its Transmission
Protection Instrument, which would eﬀectively mean restarting

quantitative easing. Additionally, some slight narrowing in eurozone-
Japan interest rates might encourage Japanese investors to increase
hedge ratios on euro-denominated debt holdings, which would be a
EUR/JPY negative.
Yen or euro the preferred funding currency? Inevitably, there will be
periods in 2027 when cross-market volatility declines and investors
again return to the carry trade. But what will the preferred funding
currency be in the G10 space? The Swiss franc has the lowest borrowing

rates, but Japanese rates should be closing in on euro rates by the end
of 2027. At that point, we could be looking at the BoJ policy rate near
1.75-2.00% and the ECB rate at 2.50%. Unless eurozone growth sharply
surprises on the upside and there is a substantial rotation into euro-
denominated asset markets, we suspect investors will be considering
euro-funded carry trades given the nature of European political risk in
2027. Assuming inﬂation does turn lower through 2027, we expect the

ECB to be more welcoming of a weaker euro than Japan is of a weaker
yen. Overall, we see a relatively ﬂat EUR/JPY through 2027 but, again,
with greater risk to the downside.
EUR/GBP: Let’s not get carried away
Your privacy is important

### Page 8

Spot Year ahead bias 4Q26 1Q27 2Q27 3Q27 4
EUR/GBP 0.84 Mildly Bullish 0.83 0.83 0.84 0.85
Sterling has overshot rate diﬀerentials: EUR/GBP is already trading
substantially lower than interest rate diﬀerentials would suggest. Some
of that is probably down to a risk premium in the euro on the back of
French budgetary risk. Sterling may also be playing a limited role as a
safe haven at the moment, given it trades at lower volatility than many
G10 FX pairs, oﬀers high interest rates and has a powerful ﬁscal rule,

which means that in 2027 the UK budget deﬁcit should narrow to close
to 3% of GDP. At the same time, we think ongoing merger and
acquisition inﬂows to the UK are playing a role here too. £141bn of deal
ﬂows have already been completed year-to-date, and there’s another
£260bn of pending or proposed inﬂows. That said, historic correlations
with yield spreads suggest EUR/GBP should be trading closer to 0.86,
meaning that current levels are stretched.

Low volatility to continue: The current analyst consensus for EUR/GBP
is 0.86 for end-2027, with a range of forecasts stretching from 0.82 to
0.91. One of the biggest trends in this pair over recent years has been
the structural decline in volatility following the political turbulence
surrounding Brexit. One-year implied volatility is trading below 5%, with
realised volatility trading well below 4%. That low volatility and the
relatively high cost of hedging are presumably keeping hedge ratios low

on sterling receivables. Assuming that the eurozone and UK economies
move broadly in line next year, we see both growing at around 1.2%
and doubt volatility needs to pick up markedly. The key threat to this
benign environment is probably political, where risks could emerge
from both sides of the Channel.
Unwinding Brexit? One wild card for the pound is the risk that Prime
Minister Andy Burnham opts for early elections next year. Labour is
performing well in the polls, and long-term government objectives on

social care and getting closer to Europe look like they will require a
mandate from the British electorate. How close the Labour Party wants
to get to Europe remains to be seen. At present, Labour policy wants
merely to start a conversation on the subject. But were Labour to put
another referendum in its election manifesto, sterling would take a lot
more positive notice. That said, an early election might not be a one-
sided risk for sterling if, say, the ﬁscal rule were to come up for

renegotiation.
EUR/CHF: SNB more tolerant of franc strength

### Page 9

Spot Year ahead bias 4Q26 1Q27 2Q27 3Q27
EUR/CHF 0.92 Mildly Bearish 0.91 0.91 0.90 0.90
Franc plays important role in SNB policy: Having proved a popular
funding currency during the summer, the rebound in the franc and the
drop in EUR/CHF have been driven by lower core rates and the re-
emergence of French budgetary risk. So far, the Swiss National Bank
(SNB) has sounded happy to keep the policy rate at zero even while the
rest of the world is tightening. Our house call remains for the SNB to

keep rates unchanged, and we doubt it would tighten even if inﬂation
nudged above 2% for a couple of months. However, much lower
inﬂation in Switzerland than in its trading partners means that the real
franc has been weakening. The SNB does not want the extra monetary
stimulus of a weaker real exchange rate right now, and we suspect it
will be happy with some nominal franc appreciation. We are not calling
for a repeat of the FX selling by the SNB in 2022-2023 but saying that

another run at 0.90 in EUR/CHF may actually suit the central bank.
Less concern over a strong franc: The minutes from the most recent
SNB meeting in September reported that companies were mentioning
the Swiss franc a lot less in their conversations with central bank
agents. In the past, the SNB had come under a lot of pressure from the
corporate sector over a strong franc, but these concerns seem to have
eased. This may be a function of the strong Swiss economy this year,

where growth is expected to come in at 1.5-2.0% this year and 1.5%
next year. The pharma sector has been a big contributor here and is
seen exporting around $115bn in 2026. Our sector experts expect a
compound annual growth rate (CAGR) of 4.5% until 2030, helped in part
by biologics. Also helping the sector are the exemptions from the 12.5-
15% US tariﬀs on the sector struck by major Swiss pharma companies
in return for investment in US manufacturing.

A preferred safe haven: The franc strengthened its credentials as a safe
haven during both 2025 and 2026. A solid AAA-rated issuer, twin
surpluses and a net foreign asset position worth over 100% of GDP
make the franc a go-to choice in the event of either a bond sell-oﬀ or
European political risk, both of which are likely to be present in 2027. At
the same time, the SNB has backed away from its "increased
willingness to intervene", a phrase introduced when EUR/CHF brieﬂy

broke below 0.90 in March. In reality, that threat had overstated the
SNB’s intentions, given that it only bought CHF4bn in the ﬁrst quarter
and under CHF2bn in the second. We therefore expect EUR/CHF to stay

oﬀered through much of 2027, with a risk of a break below 0.90 in the
event of a crisis.
EUR/SEK: Gliding back to pre-war levels
Spot Year ahead bias 4Q26 1Q27 2Q27 3Q
EUR/SEK 11.05 Mildly Bearish 11.00 10.90 10.90 10
Riksbank’s tightening cycle should be limited: The Riksbank has clearly
communicated that its baseline is for rates to reach 2.25-2.50% from
the current 2.0% by mid-2027. We agree, and while the risks remain
ﬁnely balanced between one and two more hikes, we are sticking to our

call for just one in February or March after the latest inﬂation numbers
materially undershot expectations. Still, our forecasts point to some
upside risk to the Riksbank’s 2027 inﬂation projections, with CPIF
excluding energy and temporary ﬁscal measures - the central bank’s
favoured measure - potentially peaking at 2.5-2.7% in 2Q, compared
with its 2.3% baseline. Combined with a still-solid Swedish growth
outlook, that makes our Riksbank call one of the most exposed to

hawkish revisions in the coming months. Importantly, we don’t expect
conditions for a rate cut to emerge before 2028 and estimate the long-
run neutral rate at 2.25%.
Medium- and short-term factors point to a moderate EUR/SEK
decline: Rate diﬀerentials have been the name of the game recently for
EUR/SEK. In line with this FX Outlook’s call, we think this will remain the
case next year and prove a net negative for the pair. The two-year
overnight index swap (OIS) rate gap is currently around 40bp, down

from a peak of around 60bp in September. We expect the gap to narrow
by 15-30bp over the course of 2027. The range reﬂects whether the
Riksbank hikes once or twice, as well as markets switching more quickly
to pricing in ECB rate cuts. We expect one last ECB hike in December
2026 and a cut in the fourth quarter of 2027. The growth diﬀerential
should also remain moderately negative for EUR/SEK. We estimate
2027 growth at 2.1% in Sweden versus 1.2% in the eurozone. That

should favour a gradual return to pre-war ranges in the pair.
Risks to our baseline: We see EUR/SEK stabilising around 11.00 into
end-2026, before gradually returning to 10.80 in 2027. Risks of a bigger
decline stem more from the euro side, as a busy eurozone political
calendar could drive further widening in European government bond
spreads and, in turn, looser ECB policy. Upside risks are mostly
geopolitical. Our baseline assumes Brent returns to $70-80/bbl in 2027,

a clearly optimistic scenario. SEK is less exposed to higher oil prices
now, given the Riksbank’s readiness to tighten, but a prolonged Gulf
conﬂict would still argue for a more supported EUR/SEK. The inﬂation
ramiﬁcations are particularly important: a return to a 2022-style global
inﬂationary crisis can cause SEK losses to exceed those of 2026. A
bigger threat to the krona is a potential escalation between Russia and
NATO, given Sweden’s geographical exposure. An AI bust also poses

upside risks for EUR/SEK if contagion spreads across European markets,
though SEK should be better protected in a US-idiosyncratic event
thanks to capital repatriation ﬂows.
EUR/NOK: A year for stabilisation
Spot Year ahead bias 4Q26 1Q27 2Q27 3Q27
EUR/NOK 10.59 Neutral 10.65 10.70 10.70 10.80
Cuts to start in 2H27: We think the bar for another Norges Bank hike is
fairly high. While Norway had an inﬂation problem before the Iran war,
policy was already restrictive at the start of 2026. Policymakers aren’t

ruling out another move but have sounded content with the current
4.5% rate and are mindful of overtightening risks. We broadly agree
with Norges Bank’s latest projections, which see headline and
underlying (ATE) inﬂation falling steadily below 3.0% from 1Q27 and
stabilising around 2.3-2.8% in the second half of next year. Restrictive
policy even before the energy shock, alongside krone appreciation,
limits the risk of further inﬂation overshooting. Policymakers will be

wary of jumping the gun on easing, but a roughly 2% positive real rate
will argue for some unwinding of tightening in 2H27. We forecast two
25bp cuts, in 3Q and 4Q, taking rates to 4.0%. Some growth softening
should contribute to the dovish shift: we expect 1.2% growth in 2027,
with downside risks.
A quieter year for NOK: Markets aren’t fully pricing in rate cuts in
Norway before 2028, so we expect a dovish repricing to weigh on the
krone. At the same time, we doubt Norges Bank will signal a rapid

cutting cycle ahead, as inﬂation will still be above the 2% target in
2027. Incidentally, our view leans on the dovish side of consensus, and
the risks are skewed towards a delay in easing plans. In the ﬁrst half of
the year, anyway, NOK will continue to enjoy a very attractive carry. If
our call for low volatility materialises, carry should limit the size of any
EUR/NOK rally fuelled by lower energy prices. As we see the ECB shift to
easing from 4Q27, the front-end rate diﬀerential in EUR/NOK should turn

only moderately more supportive for the pair. Expectations for lower

### Page 12

interest rates in the US too should also make for a broadly supportive
environment for the low-liquidity NOK. Finally, NOK’s stronger
fundamentals relative to the euro argue against large EUR/NOK
appreciation.
Geopolitics and equities the main risks: While our baseline is for a
quiet year for EUR/NOK, the krone is the least liquid currency in G10,
leaving it highly exposed to violent corrections in equities, like those
seen in autumn 2026. Geopolitical developments will remain a key

source of volatility risk for EUR/NOK. Any re-escalation and prolonged
conﬂict in the Middle East should bring initial beneﬁts to the krone if
energy prices ﬁnd a new, higher ﬂoor. But if inﬂation causes another
round of monetary tightening across the globe, expect NOK to
underperform, as liquidity conditions drying up and more elevated
volatility would nullify the krone’s rate advantage. The biggest black
swan for NOK downside remains any deterioration in Russia-NATO

relations.
EUR/DKK: Moderately stronger for longer
Spot Year ahead bias 4Q26 1Q27 2Q27 3Q27 4Q27
EUR/DKK 7.48 Neutral 7.47 7.47 7.47 7.47 7.46
Greater tolerance for EUR/DKK upside: Danmarks Nationalbank (DN)
has signalled greater tolerance for EUR/DKK appreciation this year.
Between October 2019 and January 2020, before the Covid impact,
levels around 7.470-7.475 prompted DKK17.8bn of FX sales to defend
the krone. Similar levels have prevailed throughout this year, with peaks

above 7.476, yet intervention has been much smaller at DKK6.3bn,
spread between June and August. The deviations from the 7.460 peg
target remain very small, reaching around 0.22% at the intraday peak
in July. Still, the signal points to a partial change in the central bank’s
tolerance for EUR/DKK upside drifts.
Intervention to remain ﬁrst line of defence: For some time, we sensed
that contained FX intervention might have been a prelude to larger DN

rate increases versus the ECB, providing structural support to the krone.
Instead, the bar for a rate reaction by DN seems to have been set
higher. Incidentally, even the small FX sales seen this year have been
enough to take the edge oﬀ EUR/DKK, indicating good eﬀectiveness.
Denmark's FX reserves have plateaued in USD terms in 2026, but at
around $110bn (approximately 22% of GDP), they still provide very
large ammunition to protect the DKK peg. We therefore expect FX

intervention to remain the ﬁrst line of defence against EUR/DKK upward
volatility. That could be associated with renewed US-Denmark
diplomatic clashes over Greenland, although the midterm blow to
President Trump has probably reduced that risk.
Upward drift can linger for longer: In our view, the +40bp policy rate
diﬀerential between the ECB and DN should prevent EUR/DKK from
trading back below central peg parity (7.460) next year. Unless DN
closes that gap through surprise rate moves, we expect EUR/DKK to

remain broadly supported in a 7.465-7.475 range for most of 2027.
Ultimately, we feel that a narrower policy rate gap is a realistic longer-
term expectation (2028 is our best guess), which should anchor
EUR/DKK back to 7.460 more steadily.
USD/CAD: A decent outlook for the loonie
Spot Year ahead bias 4Q26 1Q27 2Q27 3Q27 4Q27
USD/CAD 1.42 Bearish 1.41 1.39 1.37 1.35 1.34
BoC to remain cautious: We expect only one more Bank of Canada
(BoC) hike, likely in 1Q27, taking the policy rate to 2.75%. We admit

there are risks of an additional increase to 3.0%, but for now we view
rates at that level as unlikely. Core inﬂation has remained anchored at
2.0% since the start of the Iran war, despite US tariﬀs. The risk of
second-round eﬀects has increased, as lagged supply chain inﬂation
can start to feed through in early 2027, but some factors should keep
these eﬀects relatively contained in our view. Unemployment has
edged lower in 2026, but this reﬂects a further decline in the

participation rate rather than employment strength, with net
employment modestly contracting. Businesses continue to report
muted hiring conditions and no major pickup in expected wage growth,
which has incidentally fallen in 2026 according to oﬃcial data. Trade-
related uncertainty is also weighing on sentiment, while growth
momentum should slow in the new year, limiting businesses' ability to
pass through higher producer prices.
Front-end to favour USD/CAD softening: We expect the Fed to start

cutting rates in late 2027, dragging front-end USD rates markedly lower.
CAD short-term rates are the most correlated with the US in G10, which
often means the loonie lags most other peers during a dovish Fed
repricing. Still, BoC expectations are not aggressive, with markets
currently embedding another 50bp of tightening over the next year,
similar to the USD curve. Crucially, we estimate Fed rates are currently

around 75bp above neutral, broadly in line with FOMC dot plot
projections. In contrast, the midpoint of BoC staﬀ neutral rate estimates
is 2.75%, arguing against any monetary policy easing even if the Fed
starts cutting in late 2027. Improved growth in 2027, also thanks to a
pick-up in AI-related investment, should also play a role. We expect the
USD/CAD 2yr swap rate gap to retighten to pre-war levels, warranting a
gradual decline to 1.34 by end-2027.

Trump remains the big risk for CAD: Markets have largely dismissed the
escalation in the US-Canada trade spat in 2026, with the negative
impact on CAD remaining very contained. The US midterm result may
suggest that the widely unpopular trade war with Canada will be
shelved, increasing the chance of a comprehensive deal ahead of the
yearly United States-Mexico-Canada Agreement (USMCA) review due in
July 2027. Our baseline is for a new USMCA deal to be agreed this year,

although negotiations may be complicated and potentially involve
threats of new US tariﬀs on Canada. This may make for a bumpy path
towards an eventual CAD-positive outcome. Elsewhere, CAD may
emerge as a commodity currency of choice should geopolitical or trade
risks ﬂare up in Europe or Asia. Conversely, the risk of an AI bust could
hit CAD more than its European peers in our view, given the Canadian
economy's exposure to US activity.
AUD/USD: RBA cuts to limit upside

Spot Year ahead bias 4Q26 1Q27 2Q27 3Q27 4
AUD/USD 0.68 Mildly Bullish 0.69 0.69 0.70 0.70
RBA to shift to cuts in 2H27: We think the Reserve Bank of Australia
(RBA) is done with hikes after having taken the policy rate to 4.85%, the
highest in the G10. While policymakers haven’t entirely shut the door to
further tightening, the bar looks high as growth and housing concerns
strengthen the dovish case. Restrictive ﬁnancial conditions, housing
weakness and softer conﬁdence should weigh on domestic demand,

while data centre investment is unlikely to provide a suﬃcient oﬀset.
We expect unemployment to reach 4.8-5.0% in 2027 and rise further in
2028. We think that will lead to a shift to easing earlier than most other
central banks, with 25bp cuts in both 3Q27 and 4Q27. That would be the
start of a substantial easing cycle in our view, leading to a terminal rate
of 3.60% by end-2028. While the Australian dollar is likely to start the
year with some of the best risk-adjusted carry in the G10, its rate

attractiveness should deteriorate throughout the year.

### Page 15

Fundamentals favour stable outlook: We forecast the rate impact to be
a modest net-positive for AUD/USD, though. Markets are not pricing in
any extra tightening by the RBA, while already factoring in a cut by
end-2027. Instead, the USD curve is far more mispriced on the hawkish
side, in our view. We expect a roughly +20bp move in the AUD/USD
short-term rate diﬀerential over the next 12 months, which is
consistent with a sustainable return to 0.71 by the end of the year.

However, we expect AUD to lag many G10 peers, especially in the
second half of the year when RBA cuts will dent its carry attractiveness.
We also see Australia’s terms of trade moderating lower, in line with our
view for lower oil, natural gas and iron ore prices. On the latter, our
commodities team forecasts an average of $90-95/t on the back of new
supply from West Africa hitting the market.
AI the biggest risk for AUD: The Aussie dollar’s main vulnerability is

undoubtedly the external risk environment, especially the threat of a
tech-led equity selloﬀ. Recent episodes have consistently seen AUD
underperform most other G10 currencies, and we expect its high
implied yield to keep the Aussie a high-beta currency throughout next
year. We therefore see less scope for AUD to beneﬁt from idiosyncratic
US asset underperformance, particularly compared with low yielders.
The Aussie also remains exposed to any deterioration in China-related

sentiment. While our baseline view for China is growth stabilisation, any
renewed Sino-America trade tensions or geopolitical risk emerging in
Asia could trigger a material proxy-response by AUD.
NZD/USD: Ample room for recovery
Spot Year ahead bias 4Q26 1Q27 2Q27 3Q27 4Q2
NZD/USD 0.56 Bullish 0.56 0.57 0.58 0.59 0.60
Two more hikes by RBNZ: The Reserve Bank of New Zealand (RBNZ)
surprised with a split-vote hold in October, when markets and
consensus were fully aligned for a hike. Half of the committee holds a

rather pessimistic view on domestic and international growth prospects
and will be wary of overtightening. That view was partly validated by a
soft 3Q jobs market report. That said, the inﬂation picture continues to
argue for higher rates, in our view. We see upside risks to the RBNZ’s
latest inﬂation projections that show headline inﬂation gliding back to
2.1% by end-2027. The Bank’s projections already embed one hike from
the current 2.75% and split chances of another afterwards. Our call is

for a hike in December and another in March, reaching a 3.25%
terminal rate. The risks are skewed to a later resumption of hikes, but

prevent at least two hikes. Importantly, we don’t currently expect any

Rate support should improve for NZD: We expect a material decline in
USD front-end rates. The Kiwi dollar’s swap curve is also more hawkish
than our expectations, embedding 3.50% for one year ahead and even
another hike two years ahead. At some point in 2027, we think market
expectations for the RBNZ will be revised lower and will start to price in
a long-term neutral rate around 3.0-3.25%. Still, there should be a
material improvement in the short-term rate diﬀerential for NZD/USD,

which should drive spot back to 0.60 next year, in our estimates. The
starting point is incidentally one of short-term undervaluation, with the
pair’s recent selloﬀ larger than warranted by market fundamentals.
NZD would remain a low-yielding alternative to AUD, therefore limiting
idiosyncratic strength, but the gap should start closing as we see
markets pricing in a substantial RBA rate-cutting cycle from 2H27, while
the RBNZ’s lower starting point should curb dovish speculation. We

expect a return to the 1.15-1.20 range in AUD/NZD.
Double exposure to geopolitics and AI: The Kiwi dollar will remain
vulnerable to escalations in the Middle East due to its net-negative
exposure to higher energy prices. Any deterioration in China-related
sentiment remains another risk, although we’d expect a slightly smaller
impact on NZD than AUD, given Australia’s higher dependency on
Chinese demand. We have a similar assessment with regard to an AI-

bust scenario. NZD would be among the worst performers given its high
beta to risk sentiment, but AUD may suﬀer bigger losses as the RBA has
further room to cut in an adverse environment. We think the upside
risks to our NZD baseline forecast mostly stem from the domestic
activity/inﬂation story in New Zealand proving much stronger than
expected, leaving greater room for the RBNZ to tackle elevated
inﬂation with interest rate increases.

Document 409: NZ総選挙は与党連合が辛うじて過半数確保も、政権の行方は流動的 ～政権の枠組み次第では金融政策の目標が見直される可能性、ＮＺ_22dd47d2

Source PDF: all report pdf/NZ総選挙は与党連合が辛うじて過半数確保も、政権の行方は流動的 ～政権の枠組み次第では金融政策の目標が見直される可能性、ＮＺ_22dd47d2.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年 11 月 9 日

1/5 
NZ 総選挙は与党連合が辛うじて過半数確保も、政権の行方は流動的 
～政権の枠組み次第では金融政策の目標が見直される可能性、ＮＺドル相場にも不透明感～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ ニュージーランドでは、11 月７日に議会総選挙が実施され、 暫定結果では国民党を中心とす
る与党３党が合計67 議席を獲得し、過半数をわずか１議席上回った。ただし、約40 万票の
特別票が未集計であり、11 月27 日の公式結果の公表まで政権の行方は不透明な状況が続く。
今回の選挙では、物価高による生活費負担の増大や雇用環境の悪化、景気回復の遅れなどを
背景に、既存の二大政党への支持が低下し、中小政党が存在感を強めた。 
○ ニュージーランド準備銀行 （RBNZ）は10 月の定例会合で、 市場予想に反して政策金利を2.75％
に据え置いた。インフレ率が目標レンジを上回る一方、雇用環境の悪化や景気減速への懸念
が強まっており、金融政策の判断は難しさを増している。また、総選挙後に労働党を中心と
する政権が成立した場合には、金融政策の目標に雇用の最大化を再び加える「デュアル・マ
ンデート」が復活する可能性がある。ただし、政策目標の変更が直ちに金融緩和につながる
とは限らず、中銀の独立性を維持した政策判断が重要となる。 
○ 先行きは、12 月９日の次回会合で追加利上げが検討される可能性があるものの、 その判断は
インフレ圧力の持続性や景気・雇用動向に左右されよう。ＮＺドル相場は、RBNZ の追加利上
げ観測が下支え要因となる一方、政権の枠組みを巡る不透明感や豪州との金利差拡大などが
重石となり、当面は上値の重い展開が続くと見込まれる。さらに、景気の下振れや世界的な
リスク回避姿勢の強まりによっては、 ＮＺドル安が一段と進む可能性にも注意が必要である。

【総選挙は与党連合が過半数を暫定的に確保も、政権の行方は依然として流動的】 
ニュージーランドでは、11 月７日に議会（代議院：一院制）の総選挙が実施された。同国では、
小選挙区比例代表併用制（ＭＭＰ）が採用されており、原則として政党票の得票率に応じて議席が
配分される一方、 単独政党が過半数を獲得することは難しく、 複数政党による連立政権が形成され
てきた。 今回の総選挙を巡っては、2023 年に発足したラクソン首相率いる国民党、ＡＣＴ党、ＮＺ
ファースト党の中道右派連立政権が継続するか、 ヒプキンス党首率いる最大野党・労働党を中心と
する政権への交代が実現するかが焦点となった。

### Page 2

2/5 
選挙管理委員会が公表した暫定集計によれば、政党票の得票率は労働党が 29.36％、国民党が
29.21％と、労働党がわずかに上回った。
一方で、暫定的な議席配分では、国民党
が 42 議席、労働党が36 議席となったほ
か、緑の党が16 議席、ＡＣＴ党が13 議
席、ＮＺファースト党が12 議席、 新興の
オポチュニティ党が７議席、マオリ党が
５議席となった（図１）。 国民党、ＡＣＴ
党、ＮＺファースト党の与党３党は合計
67 議席を獲得する計算となり、 暫定的な
総議席数131 議席における過半数 （66 議
席）をわずか１議席上回った。ただし、
現時点では在外投票をはじめとする特別票 （約 40 万票）が未集計であり、11 月27 日の公式結果の
発表まで、最終的な議席配分は確定しない。このため、与党連合による政権維持が確実になった訳
ではなく、各党間の連立協議や新興政党の対応も含め、政治情勢の不透明感が続くであろう。 
今回の選挙では、国民党が政党票の得票率に基づく配分議席を上回る 42 小選挙区を獲得したた
め、７つの超過議席が発生した。マオリ党についても４つの超過議席が生じ、議会の総議席数は定
数の 120 議席から 131 議席に大幅に拡大する見通しとなっている。こうした選挙制度上の特徴が、
国民党の政党票が労働党を下回ったにもかかわらず、 与党連合が議席数で過半数を確保する要因と
なっている。一方、今回初めて議席獲得に必要な得票率５％の壁を超えたオポチュニティ党は、最
終結果次第で連立協議の行方を左右する存在となる可能性がある。また、2023 年の前回総選挙で
は、 特別票の集計により国民党が暫定結果から２議席を失うなど議席配分に変化が生じており、 今
回も公式結果の公表を待つ必要がある。

【物価高と雇用環境の悪化が有権者の不満を強め、二大政党離れが進む結果に】 
今回の総選挙では、有権者の投票行動
を左右した重要な要因として、物価高に
よる生活費負担の増大と景気回復の遅れ
が挙げられる。同国経済は、2024 年から
2025 年にかけて、 高金利や内需低迷を背
景に厳しい状況に直面した。2025 年末か
ら 2026 年初めにかけて景気に持ち直し
の動きが確認されたものの、家計消費や
住宅投資の回復は鈍く、輸出関連産業と
内需関連産業の間で景況感の乖離が続い
ている。 加えて、2026 年に入って以降は、

3/5 
イラン情勢の悪化による原油価格の上昇が家計の実質購買力を圧迫する要因となった。 こうしたな
か、７-９月のインフレ率は前年比＋3.7％と、前期（同＋4.1％）から鈍化したものの、RBNZ の目
標レンジ（１～３％）を上回っている（図２）。さらに、７-９月の失業率は5.7％と約 11 年ぶりの
高水準となるなど、家計を取り巻く環境は厳しさを増している。 
こうした経済環境は、 有権者の政治に対する評価に影響を与えたと考えられる。 今回の暫定結果
では、国民党と労働党の得票率の合計が６割を下回った一方、緑の党やＡＣＴ党、ＮＺファースト
党、オポチュニティ党などが支持を広げた。これは、既存政党の経済運営に対する不満に加え、有
権者の政策志向が多様化していることを示唆している。とはいえ、 各党の支持拡大の背景は一様で
はなく、経済・財政政策の違いに加えて、環境問題、移民政策、先住民マオリの権利なども投票判
断に影響したとみられる。また、選挙管理委員会の推計による投票率は 74.6％と、前回の 78.2％
を下回った。今回の結果は、与党に対する評価だけでなく、野党を含む既存の二大政党に対する有
権者の評価が分かれたことを反映したものと捉えられる。

【RBNZは10 月会合で予想外の金利据え置き、インフレと景気減速リスクの間で難しい判断に直面】 
RBNZ による金融政策も総選挙の重要な争点のひとつとなった。RBNZ は、2026 年７月と９月の定
例会合において、それぞれ 25bp の利上げを決定し、政策金利であるオフィシャルキャッシュレー
ト（ＯＣＲ）を2.75％まで引き上げてき
た。しかし、10 月の定例会合では、事前
には幅広く 25bp の追加利上げが予想さ
れていたにもかかわらず、政策金利を据
え置く決定を行った（注１） （図３）。こ
の決定を巡っては、７人の金融政策委員
のうち、ブレマン総裁ら４人が据え置き
を支持する一方、 残る３人が25bp の利上
げを主張する僅差の採決となった。これ
は、RBNZ の内部においても、景気への影
響を見極めるかを巡って判断が分かれて
いることを示唆している。 
RBNZ が追加利上げを見送った背景には、 物価上昇圧力が続く一方、 その持続性について慎重に判
断する必要があるとの認識がある。10 月の定例会合後に発表された声明文では、７-９月のインフ
レ率が事前予想を下回り、 賃金上昇率も抑制されており、 中期的なインフレ期待も安定しているこ
とを指摘した。 さらに、 ７月と９月に実施した利上げの影響を見極める必要があるとの見方が示さ
れた。一方、基調的なインフレ率は目標レンジの上限付近まで高まり、価格上昇が幅広い品目に広

注１ 10 月28 日付レポート「RBNZ の予想外の利上げ見送りで、NZ ドル相場は一段と弱含むか」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666951.html）

がっていることに警戒感を示した。 加えて、イラン情勢の悪化による原油価格の高止まりやＮＺド
ル安が輸入物価を押し上げることから、10-12 月のインフレ率は前年同期比＋4.4％に再加速する
と予想している。 このため、RBNZ は実際の政策判断については今後の経済・物価動向を見極める姿
勢を示した。

【総選挙後の政権の枠組み次第では金融政策の目標が見直される可能性も、RBNZの独立性が重要に】 
総選挙後の金融政策を考えるうえで重要なのが、RBNZ の政策目標を巡る与野党間の考え方の違
いであろう。 国民党を中心とする現政権は、2023 年に金融政策の目標から最大限持続可能な雇用の
達成を削除し、物価安定に重点を置く政策枠組みへと変更した。一方で、労働党は今回の総選挙の
公約に、物価安定と最大限持続可能な雇用の達成を併せて重視する「デュアル・マンデート」の復
活を掲げた。このため、 新政権の構成次第では、RBNZ の政策運営を巡る制度的な枠組みが再び見直
される可能性がある。 
とはいえ、 政策目標の見直しが直ちに利下げや金融緩和につながるとは限らない。 仮に労働党を
中心とする政権が成立し、 最大限持続可能な雇用の達成 という目標が復活した場合 においても、
RBNZ には物価安定を維持する責務が引き続き課される。 インフレ率が目標レンジを上回るなか、雇
用環境の悪化を考慮しながらも、物価上昇圧力の持続性を慎重に見極める必要がある。また、政権
交代に伴う財政政策の変更が需要やインフレ期待に与える影響によっては、 政策金利の引き下げ余
地が制約される可能性もある。一方、国民党を中心とする連立政権が継続する場合、財政規律の維
持によるインフレ抑制効果が期待されるものの、 歳出抑制が家計消費や雇用の回復を鈍らせる可能
性もある。いずれの政権が成立しても、金融政策は政府の意向ではなく、中銀が経済・物価見通し
に基づいて独立して判断するという基本原則が重要となる。 
10 月会合での金利据え置き決定を巡っては、 総選挙直前のタイミングであったため、 その政治的
な影響にも関心が集まった。 ブレマン総裁は定例会合後の記者会見において、 総選挙の日程は金融
政策の判断に影響していないと説明し、 政策目標の達成に必要な経済情勢の評価を優先していると
強調した。このため、金利据え置きの決定を選挙対策や政治的配慮によるものとみなすことは適切
ではない。 金融政策委員会の見解が僅差で分かれたことそのものが、 インフレ抑制と景気への配慮
の間で政策判断が難しくなっていることを示唆している。

【RBNZは12 月会合で追加利上げを再検討か、NZドル相場は政治情勢と金利見通しに左右される】 
先行きの金融政策については、12 月９日に予定される次回会合が重要な節目となる。RBNZ は 10
月会合において、 今後公表される経済指標が政策判断を左右する可能性を指摘しており、 追加利上
げの選択肢を維持している。金融市場の見通しも、12 月会合での 25bp の利上げを見込む向きがあ
り、2027 年にかけて政策金利が 3.25～4.00％程度まで引き上げられるとの予想が示されている。
とはいえ、足元のインフレ圧力が一時的な供給要因にとどまる場合には追加利上げを急ぐ必要性が
低下する一方で、 輸入物価の上昇が賃金やサービス価格に波及する場合には、 さらなる金融引き締
めが必要となる可能性がある。

### Page 5

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
5/5 
金融市場においては、ＮＺドル相場に下押し圧力がかかる動きがみられる。また、10 月会合での
予想外の金利据え置きを受けて、 ＮＺド
ルは対米ドルで急落した（図４）。先行き
のＮＺドル相場については、RBNZ の追加
利上げ観測が下支え要因となる一方、 政
権の枠組みを巡る不透明感が上値を抑
える可能性がある。 国民党を中心とする
与党連合が最終的に過半数を確保でき
ない場合には、 連立協議の長期化や政策
運営の不透明感が意識されやすくなる。
さらに、 オーストラリア準備銀行 （ＲＢ
Ａ）が 11 月会合で政策金利を4.85％に
引き上げたことで、両国の政策金利差が拡大していることも、 対豪ドルでＮＺドルの上値を抑える
要因となり得る（注２）。 
短期的にはＮＺドル相場の上値が重い展開が続くと見込まれる。先行きについては、 インフレ圧
力が持続して RBNZ による利上げ観測が強まれば、ＮＺドルが下支えされる可能性はある。政治情
勢の安定化によって不確実性が低下した場合には、これまでの調整の反動も重なり、 ＮＺドル相場
の持ち直しにつながることも考えられる。一方で、雇用環境が一段と悪化するとともに、景気が下
振れして追加利上げ観測が後退すれば、ＮＺドル安が一段と進む可能性もある。加えて、イラン情
勢の悪化に伴う原油高など、世界的なリスク回避姿勢が強まる場合には、 国内金利の上昇にもかか
わらずＮＺドル安が進む可能性がある点には注意が必要である。

Marion Laboure | Camilla Siazon |  STABLECOINS: What next for crypto assets?  |  October  20, 2026
STABLECOINS: What next for crypto assets?
Institutional adoption of stablecoins continues to incrementally progress in 2026. While
the frenzy from GENIUS has diminished, stablecoins’ links to the financial system have
continued to deepen, and more policymakers are seriously considering their use cases.
The market cap has hovered around ~$300bn over the past 12 months, a sign that the

stablecoin market is maturing. Stablecoin payments, while still marginal relative to
stablecoins' crypto-native purpose, are also growing, rising from around $103bn in
adjusted transaction volume in 2022 to $375bn in 2026 YTD.
For now, institutional adoption remains concentrated in cross-border payments, treasury
management, 24/7 liquidity provision, collateral, and real-time settlement.
The next big question is whether stablecoins will evolve to become merely a payments

technology, or a genuine disruptor to the global banking system. As we previously
established, the US promotion of stablecoins remains a geopolitical tool to ensure USD
dominance. Bloomberg also reported that the Trump administration is considering joint
ventures with private actors to promote USD stablecoins overseas, in a bid to boost the
dollar’s dominance and source demand for US Treasuries. Last week, Circle announced
plans to expand into 40 new markets in Southeast Asia, LatAm and the Middle East. 99%

of stablecoins (by market cap) remain pegged to USD and are largely backed by US
Treasuries.
Looking ahead, the key debate to watch is whether US regulations will allow for yield-
bearing stablecoins. Research shows that consumers say policymakers should prohibit
crypto companies from offering interest-like rewards for holding stablecoins, which will
threaten to siphon off the bank deposits that drive local lending. The US CLARITY Act
failed in Sep after lawmakers failed to agree on whether the broader crypto market

structure would support such yield-bearing stablecoins.
In this chartbook, we revisit the stablecoin market a year after the GENIUS Act was
signed. We look at: (i) how adoption has evolved, (ii) the emergence of stablecoins as
financial market plumbing via liquidity provisioning and collateral management, and (iii)
the key regulatory debates that will determine the industry’s next direction.
Payment volume has a +66% CAGR
Timeline of select events
